// Main Quest: Step 2.
rachel,182,176,3	script	Masked Girl	4_F_MASK,5,5,{
	.@char_id = getcharid(0);
	if (ep18_main == 1) {
		if (getequipid(EQI_HEAD_TOP) != 400127) {	// Ep18_Mini_Elly
			mes "有人正在大声演讲。";
			next;
			mes "[Mini Elly]";
			mes "请把它从袋子里拿出来，放到我的头上。";
			unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Mini Elly: 请把它从袋子里拿出来，放到我的头上。", bc_self;
			close;
		}
		mes "有人正在大声演讲。";
		next;
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "我收到信号，知道你已抵达本地坐标。";
		mes "这就是我叫你来的地方吗？";
		next;
		switch( select( "正确", "不" ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
			mes "没错，我们到了。";
			break;
		case 2:
			mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
			mes "我觉得不在这里。";
			next;
			mes "[Mini Elly]";
			mes "真奇怪，但这似乎符合Mini Elly目前传来的信息，我们四处看看吧。";
			break;
		}
		next;
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "访客看到什么可疑之处了吗？";
		mes "我从没见过既不是入侵者也不是访客的人，但我知道那些戴面具的人很可疑。";
		next;
		mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
		mes "那些人是这里的居民……";
		next;
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "那身装扮很有趣，不是吗？";
		mes "我会记住的。";
		next;
		mes "[Spokesman]";
		mes "...不.";
		mes "我们不是在谈论与你邻居的和平之类的事。";
		mes "我们需要的是稍微好一点的未来。";
		npctalk "演讲者：如果我们现在不行动，阿鲁纳佩尔兹的未来将陷入混乱！", "Masked Person", bc_self;
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c01" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c04" );
		npctalk "是什么？", "Crowd#ep18c01", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Spokesman]";
		mes "如果这不叫重复，那还能叫什么？";
		mes "这显然是国家分裂的迹象，我们怎么能忽视？";
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c05" );
		next;
		mes "[Spokesman]";
		mes "为什么会发生这种事？";
		mes "你为什么不能尊重对方？";
		mes "我相信每个人都有自己的想法，但我要说的是。";
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c02" );
		npctalk "是谁？", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Spokesman]";
		mes "作为这个国家的公民，把所有价值观托付给血统并对其施暴，对你来说意味着什么？";
		mes "这对你来说意味着什么？";
		cloakoffnpcself( "Nervous Priest#e18a" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c03" );
		npctalk "是谁？", "Crowd#ep18c01", bc_self;
		npctalk "把他拖出来！", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "我以前从没见过他们。他们到底是谁？", "Crowd#ep18c03", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Spokesman]";
		mes "你不觉得为这种事生气很奇怪吗？";
		npctalk "正确!", "Crowd#ep18c04", bc_self;
		npctalk "嘿，你是什么人，本地人吗！", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "需要区分这一点，不觉得奇怪吗？", "Crowd#ep18c03", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Spokesman]";
		mes "我不希望你们争斗。";
		mes "除非我们团结起来，否则没有未来。";
		npctalk "你到底想干什么！", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "这家伙没有答案。", "Crowd#ep18c04", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "演讲者完全不顾观众的声音，继续发表演讲。";
		next;
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "真有趣。有不少人戴着面具。看来即使我们都有相似的脸，也仍然能区分彼此。";
		npctalk "嘿，说得好听，但人们不断受伤并被送进医院，你觉得光说话能解决什么吗？", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "那是谁的错？", "Crowd#ep18c05", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "这个人为什么要打断说着正确话语的人？";
		next;
		mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
		mes "因为我认为那不对。";
		npctalk "你看，我以前见过这种事。发生争斗时，那只是战场吗？还是说有时移民才是唯一目标？", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "我听说是因为原住民孩子先被打了，然后他们进行了反击。", "Crowd#ep18c05", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "看来骚动越来越大了。";
		mes "每个人似乎都认为自己是对的，不愿听别人说什么。";
		npctalk "一开始就对人使用暴力是错误的！", "Crowd#ep18c05", bc_self;
		npctalk "他们一定做了什么错事。", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "我非常讨厌听到‘他们一定做了什么错事’这句话。‘错事’到底是什么意思？", "Crowd#ep18c04", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "噪音似乎比之前更大了。";
		npctalk "告诉受害者他们活该受害是错误的！", "Crowd#ep18c05", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "我们该怎么办……要介入吗？";
		npctalk "这下麻烦了……", "Nervous Priest#e18a", bc_self;
		completequest 8681;
		setquest 8682;
		ep18_main = 2;
		close;
	}

	if (ep18_main == 2) {
		mes "演讲没完没了。";
		mes "周围的气氛充满敌意。";
		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Mini Elly: It seems like there's someone here who doesn't quite fit in.", bc_self;

		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c01" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c04" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c05" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c02" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Nervous Priest#e18a" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c03" );
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main == 3 || ep18_main == 4) {
		mes "演讲没完没了。";
		mes "可怜的牧师正看向另一边。";

		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c01" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c04" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c05" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c02" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Nervous Priest#e18a" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c03" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c06" );
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main == 4) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Girl#ep18c07" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Guard Gala#ep18c08" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Guard Izu#ep18c09" );

		mes "[Masked Person]";
		mes "这里有人戴着面具演讲？";
		mes "嗯……";
		next;
		mes "[Masked Person]";
		mes "别误会，外来者可能不知道，但我们能通过这半遮面的面具认出彼此。";
		next;
		mes "[Masked Person]";
		mes "他是个勇敢的人，没有伪装。";
		mes "大概只有你分不清他和我。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Masked Person]";
	mes "Freya就是爱。";
	close;

OnTouch:
	.@char_id = getcharid(0);
	if (ep18_main == 0) {
		if (getequipid(EQI_HEAD_TOP) != 400127) {	// Ep18_Mini_Elly
			mes "蔫蔫的Elly去哪儿了？";
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	if (ep18_main == 1) {
		mes "[Mini Elly]";
		mes "请把它从袋子里拿出来，放到我的头上。";
		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Mini Elly: 请把它从袋子里拿出来，放到我的头上。", bc_self;
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main == 2 || ep18_main == 3) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c01" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c04" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c05" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c02" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Nervous Priest#e18a" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c03" );
		if (ep18_main == 3)
			cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c06" );
		end;
	}
	if (ep18_main == 4) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Girl#ep18c07" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Guard Gala#ep18c08" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Guard Izu#ep18c09" );
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8681) == 1" );
	end;
}

rachel,183,170,3	script(CLOAKED)	Nervous Priest#e18a	4_M_TRAINEE,{
	if (checkweight(1000405,20) == 0) {
		mes "- You can't proceed with the quest because you have too many items in your possession. -";
		close;
	}
	.@char_id = getcharid(0);
	if (ep18_main == 2) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c06" );
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "...?";
		mes "哦，你听见我说话了吗，抱歉。";
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "嗯……你看，最近发生了很多这类骚动。";
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "那么……冒险家是第一次来拉赫吗？";
		npctalk "我怎么会知道？你真可笑。", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "什么？这家伙还有什么说不出来的？", "Crowd#ep18c04", bc_self;
		next;
		select( "不" );
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "嗯……";
		mes "我不确定你对阿鲁纳佩尔兹了解多少，不过……";
		mes "你知道不同出身的居民之间有什么争端吗……";
		next;
		select( "我听说过" );
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "好吧，说来惭愧……这种情况还在持续。";
		mes "最近我见过一些人在发表这种煽动性演说……";
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "人们总是那样打断我。";
		mes "但你也看到了，虽然我想阻止他们，却做不到……";
		npctalk "你为什么和他争吵？", "Crowd#ep18c01", bc_self;
		npctalk "他们说争斗是无知者的行为。", "Crowd#ep18c03", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "……因为担心被视为站在统治阶级一边，那些移民。";
		mes "但这不代表你可以假装没看见……";
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "我再次紧张地看着一个从未见过的人演讲，不知不觉间……";
		npctalk "什么？", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "听起来很合适，对吧？", "Crowd#ep18c03", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "...";
		npctalk "这家伙？", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "啊，拳头飞起来了，拳头飞起来了。尽管来打我吧。", "Crowd#ep18c03", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "糟糕……";
		mes "冒险家，你有没有……";
		next;
		select( "你想让我阻止他们吗？" );
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "..May the protection of Freya be upon us.";
		changequest 8682,8683;
		ep18_main = 3;
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main == 3) {
		mes "我们该怎么办？";
		next;
		select( "请尽量安静。", "你怎么了？为什么要这样做？", "嘿，那边那个大肚子的老头！" );
		mes "[Stuffed Crowd]";
		mes "你叫谁老头？";
		npctalk "这里发生什么事了？", "Crowd#ep18c02", bc_self;
		npctalk "糟糕……", "Nervous Priest#e18a", bc_self;
		next;
		select( "怎么了？他是冒险家。" );
		mes "[Stuffed Crowd]";
		mes "咦？谁年轻时不是冒险家？";
		mes "你走过多远？我可是登上过那边托尔火山顶的人。";
		mes "不像那些脆弱的本地人。";
		npctalk "胡说，那大概只是吸引游客的噱头。", "Crowd#ep18c03", bc_self;
		npctalk "总有人喜欢吹嘘这种事。", "Crowd#ep18c05", bc_self;
		next;
		select( "我在塞斯鲁姆尼尔神殿。" );
		mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
		mes "我在塞斯鲁姆尼尔神殿，然后……";
		next;
		cloakoffnpcself( "Girl#ep18c07" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Guard Gala#ep18c08" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Guard Izu#ep18c09" );
		mes "[Girl]";
		mes "安保人员！他在那里！";
		next;
		mes "[Stuffed Crowd]";
		mes "天啊，又是谁叫了安保？";
		npctalk "啊，在事情变麻烦之前，我们快走。", "Crowd#ep18c04", bc_self;
		npctalk "希望我再也不用看到你的脸。", "Crowd#ep18c05", bc_self;
		npctalk "我还是回家吧……", "Crowd#ep18c06", bc_self;
		cloakonnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c01" );
		cloakonnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c02" );
		cloakonnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c03" );
		cloakonnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c04" );
		cloakonnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c05" );
		cloakonnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c06" );
		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Mini Elly: Are you just going to let this slide like water under the bridge?", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Guard Gala]";
		mes "我听说发生了争斗，所以过来看看……";
		mes "冒险家？";
		next;
		mes "[Nervous Priest]";
		mes "哦，不，不，不！冒险家是在帮我……";
		mes "啊，我是Junok牧师。这次我回到了塞斯鲁姆尼尔。";
		next;
		mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ".";
		mes "冒险家。";
		next;
		mes "[Guard Gala]";
		mes "谢谢你。";
		mes "最近气氛如此沉重……";
		next;
		select( "到底发生了什么？" );
		mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
		mes "究竟发生了什么？";
		mes "真没想到会发生这样的争斗……";
		next;
		mes "安保人员和牧师停了一会儿，环顾四周，交换眼神后点头表示同意。";

		mes "冒险家，你刚才在塞斯鲁姆尼尔神殿说了什么？";
		mes "我也要去神殿处理一些事，不如我们去那里聊一会儿。";
		mes "我们在神殿前继续谈吧。";

		ep18_main = 4;
		getitem 1000405,20;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
		getexp 18252408,2000000;
		navigateto("ra_temple",72,54);
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main == 4) {
		mes "[Junok]";
		mes "我马上跟上你。";
		mes "我们在<NAVI>[神殿前]<INFO>ra_temple,72,54,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>见。";
		close;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8682) == 1 || isbegin_quest(8683) == 1" );
	end;
}

ra_temple,72,54,5	script(CLOAKED)	Nervous Priest#e18b	4_M_TRAINEE,{
	if (ep18_main == 4) {
		if (getequipid(EQI_HEAD_TOP) != 400127) {	// Ep18_Mini_Elly

		cloakonnpcself( "Boksil#wms03" );

		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		end;
	}
	end;

		cloakoffnpcself( "Boksil#wms03" );

		close;
	case 2:	// shouldn't happen
		break;

		mes "[Salesman]";

			mes "[Salesman]";

			close;
		}
		if (Zeny < 550) {
			mes "[Salesman]";
			mes "你的 Zeny 不够。";
			close;
		}
		Zeny -= 550;

			erasequest 8691;
			setquest 8692;
		}
		getitem 1000410,1;	// Ep18_Water_Filter

		case 3:
			end;
		}

		mes "这是 550 Zeny~";
		next;
		if (select( "buy", "don't buy" ) == 2)
			end;
		if (Zeny < 550) {
			mes "[Salesman]";
			mes "你的 Zeny 不够。";
			close;
		}

		end;

		next;
		if (select( "I attempt maintenance work", "I stop the maintenance work" ) == 2) {
			mes "^0000ff之后想起来时再回来看看。^000000";
			close;
		}
		progressbar_npc "FFFF00",2;
		if (countitem(7197) < 1) {
			mes "没有可用于加固马车车轮的材料 <ITEM>[坚韧藤蔓]<INFO>7197</INFO></ITEM>。";
			close;
		}
		// note: Tough_Vines not deleted on fail
		.@r = rand(1,5);
		if (.@r == 1) {
			specialeffect EF_REFINEOK;
			mes "^0000ffRepair successful!^000000";
			delitem 7197,1;	// Tough_Vines
			removespecialeffect 1090;
			completequest 12532;
			close;
		}
		specialeffect EF_REFINEFAIL;
		mes "^0000ff似乎还没有正确加固。材料没有消失，所以继续尝试吧。^000000";
		close;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(12532) == 1" );
	end;
}

gw_fild01,205,102,4	script(CLOAKED)	#ep18_2_mon	EP18_GREY_WOLF,{
	if (ep18_main < 36)
		end;
	if (isbegin_quest(12534) != 1)
		end;
	.@wolf_hp = 1000;
	.@camille_hp = 1000;

	mes "^0000ff情况似乎不利。^000000";
	next;
	npctalk "Arrgghh...", "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
	next;
		npctalk "天哪", "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
	next;
	mes "^0000ff我似乎要倒下了。无法集中注意力攻击，双眼不停打转。恐怕连一秒都撑不过去，会成为狼群的美餐。^000000";
	next;
	while(1) {
		next;
		switch( select( "给 Camille 战斗建议", "我亲自处理狼", "假装没注意到并路过" ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "^0000ff告诉 Camille 攻击狼的位置？^000000";
			PlayBGM "182";
			while(1) {
				mes "Wolf vitality - ^ff0000" + .@wolf_hp + "^000000";
				mes "Camille's vitality - ^0000ff" + .@camille_hp + "^000000";
				next;
				switch( select( "胆怯攻击（高概率）", "中等攻击（普通概率）", "强力攻击（低概率）", "反击（极低概率）", "暂时逃跑" ) ) {
				case 1:
					.@wolf_chance = 65;	// 65% chance the wolf is being hit / 35% Camille
					.@wolf_damage = rand(25,50);
					.@camille_damage = rand(45,93);
					break;
				case 2:
					.@wolf_chance = 42;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(32,58);
					.@camille_damage = rand(37,72);
					break;
				case 3:
					.@wolf_chance = 30;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(45,70);
					.@camille_damage = rand(34,53);
					break;
				case 4:
					.@wolf_chance = 25;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(66,80);
					.@camille_damage = rand(26,50);
					break;
				case 5:
				mes "我很沮丧，但现在没有获胜机会。修正策略后再试一次吧。";
					close;
				}
				if (.@wolf_chance > rand(100)) {
					.@wolf_hp = .@wolf_hp - .@wolf_damage;
					npctalk "-" + .@wolf_damage, "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
					npctalk " ", "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
					specialeffect EF_ACIDDEMON, SELF, "#ep18_2_mon";
					specialeffect EF_HFLIMOON3, SELF, "#ep18_2_mon";
				}
				else {
					.@camille_hp = .@camille_hp - .@camille_damage;
					npctalk " ", "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
					npctalk "-" + .@camille_damage, "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
					specialeffect EF_ACIDDEMON, SELF, "Camille#ep18_2";
					specialeffect EF_HFLIMOON3, SELF, "Camille#ep18_2";
				}
				if (.@wolf_hp < 1) {
					mes "^0000ff你击败了灰狼。^000000";
					completequest 12534;
					cloakonnpcself( "#ep18_2_mon" );
					playbgm "183";
					close;
				}
				if (.@camille_hp < 1) {
					next;
					mes "^ff0000危险！！我必须停止战斗并恢复 Camille 的健康。^000000";
					playbgm "183";
					close;
				}
			}
			break;
		case 2:
			mes "^0000ff不，也许让 Camille 自己成长才是正确的……^000000";
			continue;
		case 3:
			mes "^0000ff稍后回来为 Camille 建墓。^000000";
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(12534) == 1" );
	end;
}

	end;
}

			mes "[艾露因]";

			close;
		}
		if (.@trap01 > 0)
			delitem 1000412, .@trap01;

			delitem 1000414, .@trap03;

	case 1:

		.@trap03 = countitem(1000414);
		if ((.@trap01 + .@trap02 + .@trap03) < 5) {

gw_fild01,24,122,0	script	Trap Set#01	CLEAR_NPC,{
	if (checkweight(1000405,20) == 0) {
		mes "- 请稍等！！-";
		mes "- 你目前携带了-";
		mes "- 太多物品。-";
		mes "- 请再试一次，-";
		mes "- 先减轻负重。-";
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main < 36)
		end;
	if (isbegin_quest(8688) == 1 || isbegin_quest(8690) == 1) {
		.@amount = countitem(1000412) + countitem(1000413) + countitem(1000414);
		if (.@amount >= 5) {
			mes "今天收集的陷阱捕获物已经够了。";
			mes "把它带给<NAVI>[艾露因]<INFO>wolfvill,202,166,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>吧。";
			close;
		}
		specialeffect2 EF_STEAL;
		progressbar "FFFF00",10;
		.@r = rand(1,5);
		if (.@r == 1)
			getitem 1000412,1;	// Ep18_Trapped01
		else if (.@r == 2)
			getitem 1000413,1;	// Ep18_Trapped02
		else if (.@r == 3)
			getitem 1000414,1;	// Ep18_Trapped03
		else
			mes "什么也没拿到……";
		disablenpc strnpcinfo(0);
		initnpctimer;
		end;
	}
	end;
OnTimer60000:
	enablenpc strnpcinfo(0);
	stopnpctimer;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_CLICKME, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8688) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_CLICKME, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8690) == 1" );
	end;
}

			completequest 16548;
			setquest 16550;
			getitem 1000405,20;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 7769124,3000000;
			mes " ";
			mes "^4d4dff 你获得了良好评价。^000000";
			add_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18, 100);
			if (get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18) >= 1000 && ep18_main == 36) {// Custom implementation: reputation 1000 on wolfvill => player can continue the main quest
				ep18_main = 37;
				setquest 18082;
				mes "^4d4dff 灰狼村的村民接受你成为村子的一员。^000000";
				mes "你有资格参加^4d4dff会议。^000000";
			}
			close;
		}
	}
	switch( checkquest(16550,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "看来" + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "仍然潜伏着。";
		mes "如果现在刺激它们，今晚可能会因为邪气而更加吵闹。";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "你会在天亮后回来捕捉一只" + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "吗？";
		mes "拜托你了，冒险者。";
		close;
	case 2:
		erasequest 16550;
		break;
	}
	switch( checkquest(16549,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "唉，今天我又困得不行。";
		mes "冒险者，你能帮我捕捉30只" + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "吗？";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "如果你成群猎捕，" + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "今晚大概就会安静了。";
		next;
		if (select( "当然，我会全部捕捉。", "我没兴趣捕捉它们。" ) == 2) {
			mes "[阿希尔]";
			mes "我想你也很忙吧，冒险者？";
			mes "你一定累了，去休息吧。不过有这么多鸣叫声，可能很难睡着。";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "哇，谢谢你，冒险者！";
		mes "一共30只" + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "，真的非常感谢。";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "全部狩猎完成后回来告诉我。我会准备一份小奖励！";
		setquest 16549;
		close;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "谢谢你，冒险家！";
		mes "这里有30只" + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "。请处理它们。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "谢谢你！风暴鸢的鸣叫声明显减弱了！";
		mes "今晚我可以舒服地睡觉了。";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "好吧，可能不会持续太久。";
		mes "明天声音还会变大，但希望你黎明后再来，抓一只" + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "。";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "这是对你让我今晚睡个好觉的感谢。";
		mes "请收下这颗小小的心，冒险家。";
		erasequest 16549;
		setquest 16550;
		add_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18, 30);
		if (get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18) >= 5000)
			.@bonus = 1;
		if (get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18) >= 1000 && ep18_main == 36) {// Custom implementation: reputation 1000 on wolfvill => player can continue the main quest
			ep18_main = 37;
			setquest 18082;
			mes "^4d4dff The villagers of the gray wolf have accepted you as part of the village.^000000";
			mes "你有资格参加^4d4dff会议。^000000";
		}
		getitem 1000405,3+.@bonus;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
		getexp 10318080,3000000;
		close;
	}
	end;

OnTouch_:
	npctalk "Ahir: I'm really tired... and need more sleep.", "Ahir#ep18", bc_self;
	end;

OnInit:
	// first completion
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16548,HUNTING) == -1 && isbegin_quest(11724) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16548,HUNTING) == 2" );

	// daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(16548) == 2 && checkquest(16549,HUNTING) == -1 && (checkquest(16550,PLAYTIME) == -1 || checkquest(16550,PLAYTIME) == 2)" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16549,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}

rachel,155,236,3	script	Junok#atm	4_M_TRAINEE,{
	if (checkweight(1000405,10) == 0) {
		mes "[Junok]";
	mes "希望情况能尽快平息。整理好随身物品后再来和我谈谈吧。";
		close;
	}
	if (BaseLevel < 170) {
		mes "[Junok]";
	mes "你好。看来你目前还缺乏完成我这个请求所需的经验。";
		next;
		mes "[Junok]";
	mes "再接受一些训练后回来找我吧。";
		next;
	mes "- 此任务仅限 170 级及以上角色接取。-";
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main < 12)
		end;	// unknown text
	switch( checkquest(3504,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:	// first time
		if (isbegin_quest(3503) == 0) {
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "你好，我是朱诺克牧师。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "这个村庄长期以来分成原住民和移民两派，双方一直处于对立状态。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "虽然一直有人努力解决两派之间的冲突，但目前还没有取得明显成果。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "因此，我想请你分别直接接触他们，了解问题究竟出在哪里。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "你能代替我和他们谈谈吗？我不方便离开自己的岗位。";
			next;
			if (select( "好的。", "下次吧。" ) == 2) {
				mes "[Junok]";
				mes "你拒绝了我，真让我失望。我的心好痛。";
				close;
			}
			setquest 3503;

			setarray .@quest_id[0],3493,3494,3495,3496,3497,3498,3499,3500,3501,3502;
			.@size = getarraysize(.@quest_id);

			for ( .@i = 0; .@i < 6; ++.@i ) {
				.@r = rand(.@size);
				setquest .@quest_id[.@r];
				deletearray .@quest_id[.@r],1;
				.@size--;
			}
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "请和指定的 3 名原住民及 3 名移民交谈，之后把结果告诉我。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "需要交谈的人员名单可以在任务窗口或小地图上查看。";
			close;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Junok]";
	mes "今天我已经收集到足够的信息了。如果还需要帮助，请明天再来。";
		close;
	case 2:	// daily
		if (isbegin_quest(3503) == 0) {
			mes "[Junok]";
		mes "好的，我今天会了解原住民和移民之间的情况。如果有具体需要我帮忙的地方，尽管告诉我。";
			next;
			if (select( "好的", "不用。" ) == 2)
				end;
			erasequest 3504;
			setquest 3503;

			setarray .@quest_id[0],3493,3494,3495,3496,3497,3498,3499,3500,3501,3502;
			.@size = getarraysize(.@quest_id);

			for ( .@i = 0; .@i < 6; ++.@i ) {
				.@r = rand(.@size);
				setquest .@quest_id[.@r];
				deletearray .@quest_id[.@r],1;
				.@size--;
			}
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "那就拜托你了。如果需要帮助，尽管来问我。祝你今天愉快！";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "和 3 名原住民及 3 名移民谈过后再回来吧。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "需要交谈的人员名单可以在任务窗口或小地图上查看。";
			close;
		}
		break;
	}
	switch( isbegin_quest(3505) ) {
	case 0:
		mes "[Junok]";
		mes "请和全部 6 名居民交谈后再回来。";
		close;
	case 1:	// task completed
		switch( checkquest(3504,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:	// first completion
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "是这样啊……";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "看来他们之间仍然积怨很深。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "比我预想的还要严重。嗯……不知道该怎么解决才好。";
			next;
			getitem 1000405,10;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 7769124,3000000;	// dummy value
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:	// (shouldn't happen)
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "今天我已经收集到足够的信息了。如果还需要帮助，请明天再来。";
			close;
		case 2:	// daily quest
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "是这样啊……";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "看来他们之间仍然积怨很深。";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "比我预想的还要严重。嗯……不知道该怎么解决才好。";
			next;
			getitem 1000405,3;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 18252408,2000000;
			break;
		}
		for ( .@i = 3493; .@i < 3506; ++.@i ) {
			if (isbegin_quest(.@i) > 0)
				erasequest .@i;
		}
		setquest 3504;
		mes "[Junok]";
		mes "这些指导很有帮助……";
		next;
		mes "[Junok]";
		mes "谢谢你的帮助。如果明天还能帮忙，请再来一趟。";
		close;
	case 2:
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep18_main >= 12 && isbegin_quest(3503) == 0 && (checkquest(3504,PLAYTIME) == -1 || checkquest(3504,PLAYTIME) == 2)" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(3505) == 1" );
	end;
}

	mes "因为原住民加薪一事没有达成妥协，我吃了不少苦。";
		next;
		mes "[Hari]";
	mes "其实我也不觉得需要加薪，但我更不喜欢和丈夫争吵。";
		next;
		mes "[Hari]";
	mes "不管怎样，看来已经有了一些妥协，我很高兴。";
		next;
		mes "[Hari]";
	mes "现在我想请你安静。";
		next;
		completequest 3502;
		for ( .@i = 3493; .@i <= 3502; ++.@i )
			.@total += isbegin_quest(.@i);
		if (.@total == 12)
			setquest 3505;
		mes "[Hari]";
	mes "那么就进去吧~";
		next;
	mes "- 和哈莉的谈话结束了。-";
		next;
	mes "- 去找下一个联系人吧。-";
		if (.@total == 12) {
			next;
	mes "- 你已经和全部6个人谈过了。回朱诺克那里吧。-";
		}
		close;
	}
	mes "[Hari]";
	mes "你好，我是哈莉。";
	next;
	mes "[Hari]";
	mes "她就是我面前那个固执又不屈服的女人。";
	close;

OnTouch_:
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(3502) == 1" );
	end;
}

		mes "- Wait a minute !! -";

	}
	if (isbegin_quest(5933) == 1) {
		if (checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) == 2)
			erasequest 5937;
		npctalk "梅杰：哦，你来了？今天也要精神饱满地开始吗？", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;

		next;

		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "我必须在这里成功。";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "我知道这并不理想，但这门生意是我用攒下的每一分钱创办的。";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "没有退路。所以我会尽力，也希望我的员工们同样努力。";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "我会努力成为以自身行动回报你们努力的老板。";
		next;
		setquest 5942;
		completequest 5942;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "开业仪式结束！现在让我们全心投入工作吧？";
		next;
	}
	switch( checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		npctalk "梅杰：好了！今天的工作结束了。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		npctalk "梅杰：回家陪伴家人吧。你不只有这一件事要做。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		end;
	case 2:
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		npctalk "梅杰：你好。今天也一起努力吧。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		break;
	}
	switch( select( "I'm currently busy..", "Harvesting Half Flowers", "Delivering Half Flowers" ) ) {
	case 1:
		npctalk "梅杰：你似乎在忙别的事？有空时来见我。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		end;
	case 2:
		for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
			if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
				.@quest_id = .@id;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (.@quest_id == 0)
			.@quest_id = rand(5938,5941);

		switch( isbegin_quest(.@quest_id) ) {
		case 0:
			npctalk "梅杰：你打算采集半花吗？", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			if (select( "I'll go collect them.", "I'll skip today." ) == 2)
				end;
			switch( checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) ) {
			case -1:
				break;
			case 0:
			case 1:
				npctalk "梅杰：哦！你确定吗？", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
				sleep2 1000;
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
				npctalk "梅杰：今天还没结束，如果你提前完成任务，也请考虑来花房帮忙。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				end;
			case 2:
				break;
			}
			setquest .@quest_id;
			npctalk "梅杰：今天请小心树旁盛开的半花。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
			sleep2 1000;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
			npctalk "梅杰：好！加油！今天也精神饱满地带回新鲜半花。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			end;
		case 1:
			if (rentalcountitem(1000407) < 1) {
				npctalk "梅杰：今天也请继续为新鲜半花努力。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				end;
			}
			delitem 1000407,1;	// Eps18_Half_Flower
			erasequest .@quest_id;
			if (checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) == 2)
				erasequest 5937;
			setquest 5937;
			getitem 1000405,3;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 18252408,2000000;
			npctalk "梅杰：今天也辛苦了。剩下的交给我吧。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			end;
		case 2:
			end;	// impossible
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		npctalk "梅杰：这项服务我还在考虑。等生意稳定一些后，也许可以开始？", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		npctalk "梅杰：到那时就拜托你帮忙了。", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5936) == 2 && isbegin_quest(5938) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5939) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5940) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5941) == 0 && checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5936) == 2 && rentalcountitem(1000407) > 0" );
	end;
}

gw_fild01,325,376,1	script	Half Flower#EP18_R01	4_EP18_HALFFLOWER,7,7,{
	if (checkweight(1000407,1) == 0) {
		mes "- Wait a minute !! -";
		mes "- Currently you're carrying -";
		mes "- too many items with you. -";
		mes "- Please try again -";
		mes "- after you loose some weight. -";
		close;
	}
	for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
		if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
			.@quest_id = .@id;
			break;
		}
	}
	if (.@quest_id == 0) {
		unittalk getcharid(3), "Half flower is blooming", bc_self;
		end;
	}
	// player interacts with the npc according to the quest ID
	// 5938 requires EP18_R (flower near a Rock)
	// 5939 requires EP18_L (?)
	// 5940 requires EP18_T (Tree)
	// 5941 requires EP18_W (Water)
	switch( .@quest_id ) {
	case 5938:
		.@string$ = "EP18_R";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],4,1,4,1;
		break;
	case 5939:
		.@string$ = "EP18_L";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],3,2,3,2;
		break;
	case 5940:
		.@string$ = "EP18_T";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],2,2,2,3;
		break;
	case 5941:
		.@string$ = "EP18_W";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],1,4,1,4;
		break;
	}
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2), .@string$) == false) {
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0), getcharid(0);
		end;
	}
	if (rentalcountitem(1000407) > 0) {
		mes "- the Half Flower is more";
		mes "- not necessary, before wither";
		mes "- Bring it to Mejai.";
		close;
	}
	.@time = 86400;
	mes "- The Half Flower is in bloom. Let's";
	mes "- gather the required materials from the";
	mes "- harvesting tool provided by Mejai.";
	next;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : What angle should I cut the stem?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("以 10° 角切割", "以 20° 角切割", "以 30° 角切割", "以 40° 角切割" ) != .@good_answer[0])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : How many leaves should I leave?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("留下 2 片叶子", "留下 4 片叶子", "留下 6 片叶子", "留下 8 片叶子" ) != .@good_answer[1])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : How many milliliters of water should I put in the small bottle?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("加入 20 毫升", "加入 30 毫升", "加入 40 毫升", "加入 50 毫升" ) != .@good_answer[2])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : How many grams of secret powder should I put in?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select( "Put in 2 grams.", "Put in 4 grams.", "Put in 6 grams.", "Put in 8 grams." ) != .@good_answer[3])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	cloakonnpcself();
	// note: the timer decreases according to the answer but no idea of the ratio
	rentitem 1000407, .@time;	// Ep18_Half_Flower
	mes "- You have harvested the Half Flower.";
	mes "- Let's deliver it to Mejai before it withers.";
	close;

OnTouch:
	for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
		if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
			.@quest_id = .@id;
			break;
		}
	}
	if (.@quest_id == 0)
		end;
	setarray .@string$[5938], "EP18_R", "EP18_L", "EP18_T", "EP18_W";
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2), .@string$[.@quest_id]) == false) {
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0), getcharid(0);
		end;
	}
	end;
}

	case 2:
		mes "[Gatekeeper]";
		mes "那去附近狩猎一些怪物怎么样？";
		mes "我睡觉时它们太吵了。";
		close;
	case 3:
		mes "[Gatekeeper]";
		mes "如你所见，我是守门人。";
		mes "我也会赶走不速之客。";
		mes "通常我伪装成流浪者，但我其实不是。";
		mes "你敢把我当成那种人！";
		close;
	case 4:
		if (ep18_main == 35) {
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "不，不。我不会刚见面就请你帮忙。";
			mes "森林可能有点危险，但没关系。";
			close;
		}
		switch( checkquest(18087,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "嗯……暂时没事。";
			mes "它确实构成了一定威胁，所以它们考虑后不会攻击。";
			close;
		case 2:
			erasequest 18087;
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(18086,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "如你所见，我在这里守卫入口。";
			mes "有时怪物一路来到这里，明明我很累还要来打扰我。";
			next;
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "能在附近森林清理约 30 只任意怪物吗？";
			mes "我有些捡到的漂亮紫水晶碎片，会给你。";
			next;
			if (select( "接受请求", "拒绝" ) == 2) {
				mes "[Gatekeeper]";
				mes "我明白了……你是不在乎可怜守门人生命安全的人……";
				mes "知道了。我会真诚地把这件事记在心里。";
				close;
			}
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "谢谢！";
			mes "现在我可以放松一下了！";
			mes "那就交给你了。";
			setquest 18086;
			close;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "什么都行。就在附近猎杀一些怪物。";
			mes "这样我守卫时也更安全，对吧？";
			close;
		case 2:
			end;
		}
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_CLICKME, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep18_main == 35" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep18_main > 35 && checkquest(18086,HUNTING) == -1 && (checkquest(18087,PLAYTIME) == -1 || checkquest(18087,PLAYTIME) == 2)" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(18086,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}

gw_fild01,147,137,2	script	Boksil#wms04	EP18_GREY_WOLF_BABY,{
	end;
OnMove:
	while(1) {
		switch( .move ) {
		case 0:
			.val = !.val;
			setarray .@a[0],147,137;
			setarray .@b[0],147,138;
			break;
		case 1:
			setarray .@a[0],148,127;
			setarray .@b[0],147,127;
			break;
		case 2:
			setarray .@a[0],159,115;
			setarray .@b[0],160,115;
			break;
		case 3:
			setarray .@a[0],159,104;
			setarray .@b[0],160,104;
			break;
		case 4:
			setarray .@a[0],159,93;
			setarray .@b[0],160,93;
			break;
		case 5:
			setarray .@a[0],160,82;
			setarray .@b[0],161,82;
			break;
		case 6:
			.val = !.val;
			setarray .@a[0],161,71;
			setarray .@b[0],162,71;
			break;
		}
		.move = .move + .sens[.val];
		unitstopwalk getnpcid(0),USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitstopwalk getnpcid(0,"Goat#wms03"),USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitwalk getnpcid(0),.@a[0],.@a[1];
		unitwalk getnpcid(0,"Goat#wms03"),.@b[0],.@b[1];
		sleep 6000;
	}
	end;
OnInit:
	npcspeed 300;
	setarray .sens[0],1,-1;
	.move = 1;
	donpcevent strnpcinfo(0) + "::OnMove";
	end;
}

// Storage and unlock reputation feature
wolfvill,167,135,3	script	Asad#asad	4_EP18_GW_MAN01,{
	if (checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
		mes "- 等一下！！-";
		mes "- 你目前携带了-";
		mes "- 太多物品。-";
		mes "- 请再试一次-";
		mes "- 减轻负重后。-";
		close;
	}
	.@reputation = get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18);
	if (.@reputation < 1000) {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "先生，好久不见。";
		mes "熟悉村子后，来找我吧。";
		mes "我会为你提供一些便利。";
		next;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "但前提是你^4d4dff要参加比赛^000000。";
		mes "我不会随便帮任何人。";
		next;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "仔细观察，就会发现有些地方需要援手。";
		mes "伸出援手，你就能赢得人们的好感。";
		mes "尽力而为吧。";
		close;
	}

	if (.@reputation >= 3000) {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "欢迎，像狼一样的 " + strcharinfo(0) + "。";
		mes "你需要什么？";
		mes "哦，我一直在寻找更好的熔炼箱。";
		mes "想用紫水晶交换吗？";
	}
	else {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "欢迎，先生。";
		mes "想整理一些东西吗？";
		mes "还是想看看我出售的物品？";
	}
	next;
	.@amethyst_name$ = getitemname(1000405);
	if (.@reputation >= 4000)
		.@menu$[2] = "用 " + .@amethyst_name$ + " 交换熔炼箱";
	else if (.@reputation >= 3000)
		.@menu$[1] = "用 " + .@amethyst_name$ + " 交换熔炼箱";
	if (.@reputation >= 2000)
		.@menu$[0] = "请求空间移动。";
		switch( select( "使用仓库。", "使用商店。", .@menu$[0], .@menu$[1], .@menu$[2] ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "仓库存储费是 200 Zeny。";
		mes "你想打开哪个仓库？";
		next;
		select( "仓库 1" );
		if (Zeny < 200)
			end;
		Zeny -= 200;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "好，我会打开仓库。";
		mes "请好好使用。";
		close2;
		openstorage();
		end;
	case 2:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "你要出售物品吗？";
		mes "你要购买需要的东西吗？";
		next;
		switch( select( "购买物品。", "出售物品。" ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "我不确定是否有你需要的一切。";
			mes "但无论是什么，你都可以信赖我。";
			close2;
			callshop "Tool Dealer#wolf",1;
			end;
		case 2:
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "好。我会以合理价格卖给你任何东西。";
			close2;
			callshop "Tool Dealer#wolf",2;
			end;
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "要用 1 个 " + .@amethyst_name$ + " 传送到拉赫吗？";
		next;
		if (select( "否", "是" ) == 1)
			end;
		if (countitem(1000405) < 1) {
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "你的材料不够。";
			close;
		}
		delitem 1000405,1;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
		warp "rachel",115,125;
		end;
	case 4:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "紫水晶商店？";
		mes "你可以用它们交换熔炼箱……";
		close2;
		callshop "barter_ep18_2",1;
		end;
	case 5:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "紫水晶商店？";
		mes "你可以用它们交换熔炼箱……";
		close2;
		callshop "barter_ep18_3",1;
		end;
	}
	end;
}
